# Evidence: .connect Annual Summary — Full Year 2020

## Source
- **File:** `2020_Connects_All.xlsx`
- **Date:** 2020-01 to 2020-12
- **Ingested:** 2026-08-05
- **Channel:** connect_tracking
- **Category:** .connect Program / Annual Engagement Summary

## Metadata
- **Period:** Full Year 2020 (February — December)
- **Total connect interactions:** 2731
- **Unique participants (full year):** 778
- **Unique cities:** 26
- **Unique job roles:** 45
- **Unique departments:** 74
- **Worksheets in source:** UniqueParticipants, UniqueCities, UniqueJobRoles, UniqueDepartments, 2020_dsvDotConnects

## Datta's Involvement
- **Role at time:** Competency Specialist — Software Excellence, SWCoE
- **Involvement type:** Program owner — conducted all connects
- **Significance:** This is a full-year longitudinal record of Datta's 1:1 engagement program across Philips engineering. 2731 individual connect interactions with 778 unique engineers demonstrates extraordinary organizational reach and influence for a single contributor.

## Annual Impact Summary
- **2731 total connect interactions** across 11 months (Feb-Dec 2020)
- **778 unique people** engaged through the .connect program
- **26 cities** across multiple countries
- **74 departments** reached across Philips
- **45 distinct job role categories** engaged
- **Average ~248 interactions/month** sustained throughout 2020

## Key Leadership Indicators
1. **Scale of influence:** 778 unique engineers is extraordinary for a non-management role
2. **Cross-functional reach:** 74 departments shows organization-wide impact
3. **Geographic span:** 26 cities demonstrates global engagement
4. **Consistency:** Sustained monthly connects from Feb through Dec 2020
5. **Depth of engagement:** Top participants had 10+ repeated connects, showing ongoing mentoring relationships
